▶ apps-script-deploy — deploy state machine: new-project happy path
  ✔ first deploy walks create→upload→version→deploy in order and probes public access without re-fetching the deployment it just created (8.186541ms)
  ✔ every Apps Script API call carries the deploy token as a Bearer header — an unauthenticated call would 401 mid-deploy (0.88825ms)
  ✔ the uploaded manifest FORCES the public webapp config (ANYONE_ANONYMOUS / USER_DEPLOYING) regardless of what the repo manifest says — otherwise the probe can never pass (2.371084ms)
  ✔ a verified deploy persists the managed state as ready and marks the agent checklist webhookConfigured (1.225875ms)
  ✔ a verified deploy adopts the /exec URL (config patch + webhook field) and records stub_only — deploy proves wiring, never a real engine (7.775417ms)
  ✔ a corrupted local manifest aborts before ANY Google API traffic — no half-created project from a broken repo file (2.372291ms)
✔ apps-script-deploy — deploy state machine: new-project happy path (23.093875ms)
▶ apps-script-deploy — re-deploy reuses the managed project and deployment
  ✔ an existing managed state skips project creation and UPDATES the deployment in place — a POST would mint a duplicate web app with a NEW /exec URL (1.472209ms)
✔ apps-script-deploy — re-deploy reuses the managed project and deployment (1.531417ms)
▶ apps-script-deploy — deploy guards fail fast before any Google traffic
  ✔ a deploy already in flight is a no-op — no duplicate token prompts or racing API writes (1.517125ms)
  ✔ missing Sheet ID stops with a warning before requesting any token — there is nothing to attach the /exec URL to (0.696417ms)
  ✔ missing OAuth client ID stops with a warning before requesting any token (19.445167ms)
  ✔ an unsaved OAuth client change aborts the token request — deploying on the OLD client would mint a token for the wrong app (0.714542ms)
  ✔ GIS not loaded yet fails honestly with 'still loading' instead of hanging the deploy (0.506167ms)
  ✔ partially granted scopes abort the deploy — a weak token would fail half-way through with a confusing Google error (0.396542ms)
  ✔ a blocked permission popup maps to actionable 'allow popups' guidance, not a raw GIS error object (2.106416ms)
✔ apps-script-deploy — deploy guards fail fast before any Google traffic (25.583542ms)
▶ apps-script-deploy — public-access verification never saves an unverified /exec URL as usable
  ✔ a deployment that came back without ANYONE_ANONYMOUS access is persisted as needs_remediation and the webhook URL is NOT adopted (0.660084ms)
  ✔ a probe answered by the WRONG service (not the command-center stub) still counts as a failure — any 200 from a login wall must not pass (0.564291ms)
  ✔ a probe script load error (Google auth wall / 403 page) routes to probe remediation (8.057542ms)
  ✔ a probe that never answers resolves via the armed timeout timer and routes to probe remediation — no infinite 'checking…' spinner (14.984625ms)
  ✔ the JSONP callback is removed from window even when the probe fails — leaked globals would collide on the next probe (0.817292ms)
✔ apps-script-deploy — public-access verification never saves an unverified /exec URL as usable (25.238584ms)
▶ apps-script-deploy — Google API errors map to actionable remediation, not raw payloads
  ✔ 'user has not enabled the Apps Script API' routes to Apps Script USER settings with the Google-provided link (0.781584ms)
  ✔ SERVICE_DISABLED routes to enabling script.googleapis.com in the OAuth client's Cloud project (0.569ms)
  ✔ a 401 mid-deploy reads as an expired Google session with retry guidance (10.901041ms)
  ✔ a network/CORS failure against the Apps Script API reads as 'could not reach', not a raw TypeError (0.756209ms)
  ✔ any other API error keeps Google's own message as the detail and harvests at most two links as actions (0.881834ms)
✔ apps-script-deploy — Google API errors map to actionable remediation, not raw payloads (14.060625ms)
▶ apps-script-deploy — recheck re-probes public access without redeploying
  ✔ recheck is a no-op without a managed deploy state — there is nothing to probe and no status churn (0.547583ms)
  ✔ recheck re-reads the deployment from Google instead of trusting the cache — access fixed in the Apps Script editor must become visible (5.627208ms)
  ✔ a recheck that passes persists ready state, preserves lastDeployedAt, and adopts the URL with the recheck reason (2.359209ms)
  ✔ a recheck that still fails the probe keeps needs_remediation and does NOT adopt the URL (0.724708ms)
✔ apps-script-deploy — recheck re-probes public access without redeploying (17.641292ms)
▶ apps-script-deploy — SerpAPI callout probes only locally reachable workers
  ✔ non-local webhook URLs skip the /health probe entirely — a relay hides the real worker health, so guessing would mislead (52.499083ms)
  ✔ a local worker is probed at /health with the query stripped, and a configured SerpAPI shows the configured badge (3.018292ms)
  ✔ a worker that does not report the flag is called out as too old instead of guessing yes/no (2.762042ms)
  ✔ an unreachable worker reads 'Worker unreachable' instead of leaving a stale badge or throwing (4.787208ms)
✔ apps-script-deploy — SerpAPI callout probes only locally reachable workers (63.367625ms)
